       DATA DIVISION.   
       SUBSCHEMA SECTION.   
       INVOKE SUBSCHEMA PST100UCOBSU
              IN FILE SUBSCHEMAS OF SCHEMA PST100SCHEMA 
144449        COPYING RECORDS    INTO COMMON
144449        COPYING DATA-NAMES INTO COMMON
144449        OVERLAY PAX-REC WITH PAX-CXF-REC  
169014*                            PAX-FUT-REC  
144449                             PAX-VI-REC   
144449        SAVE DATA INCLUDES DEFERRED-UPDATES   
144449        DMCA  COMMON  
144449        ERROR RECOVERY IS 9100-DATABASE-ERROR 
144449        ROLLBACK       IS 9100-DATABASE-ERROR.

      *    after they have been validated and reformatted.

PBS-ME*      Here's a good place to explain the meaning of each 
PBS-ME*      WS10-ACTION-IND value: 
PBS-ME*         'A' - Add a new cabin booking   
PBS-ME*         'M' - Modify a cabin booking
PBS-ME*         'R' - Reinstate a cancelled cabin booking   
PBS-ME*         'P' - Price balance a cabin booking 
PBS-ME*      Lower case values are same as upper case except that   
PBS-ME*      no update occurs; only pricing is returned.

       1120-MOVE-PAX-FIELDS.
           MOVE FUNCTION LENGTH (U-931-PAX-SEQ-NBR) 
                                     TO WS15-STR-LEN
           MOVE 1                    TO WS15-IDX
           MOVE 1                    TO WS15-STR-PTR

           PERFORM  
               UNTIL   (WS15-STR-PTR > WS15-STR-LEN)
                   OR  (WS15-IDX > WS10-NUM-PAX)

             UNSTRING                   U-931-PAX-SEQ-NBR   
                 DELIMITED BY           WS01-NEWLINE-CHR
                     OR                 WS01-TAB-CHR
                     OR                 WS01-UNDLN-CHR  
                 INTO                   DPP81-HOLD-STRING   
                 WITH POINTER           WS15-STR-PTR
             END-UNSTRING   

             PERFORM DPP81-VALIDATE-NUMVAL  
             IF  DPP81-NUMVAL-IS-VALID  

               MOVE FUNCTION NUMVAL (DPP81-HOLD-STRING) 
                                     TO WS15-GENERIC-NUMERIC
               MOVE   WS15-GENERIC-NUMERIC  
                   TO WS11-SEQ-NBR (WS15-IDX)   

               IF      (WS11-SEQ-NBR (WS15-IDX) 
                        = WS15-GENERIC-NUMERIC) 

                 CONTINUE   

               ELSE 

      *          Unexpected sign or decimal fraction.   

                 PERFORM C8807-INVLD-SEQ-NBR
               END-IF   
             ELSE   
               PERFORM C8807-INVLD-SEQ-NBR  
             END-IF 

             ADD 1                   TO WS15-IDX

           END-PERFORM  

           PERFORM  
               UNTIL (WS15-IDX > WS10-NUM-PAX)  
             MOVE   0   
                 TO WS11-SEQ-NBR (WS15-IDX) 
             ADD 1                   TO WS15-IDX
           END-PERFORM  

      *    ------------ 

           MOVE FUNCTION LENGTH (U-931-PAX-LAST-NAME)   
                                     TO WS15-STR-LEN
           MOVE 1                    TO WS15-IDX
           MOVE 1                    TO WS15-STR-PTR

           PERFORM  
               UNTIL   (WS15-STR-PTR > WS15-STR-LEN)
                   OR  (WS15-IDX > WS10-NUM-PAX)

             UNSTRING                   U-931-PAX-LAST-NAME 
                 DELIMITED BY           WS01-NEWLINE-CHR
                     OR                 WS01-TAB-CHR
                     OR                 WS01-UNDLN-CHR  
                 INTO WS11-LAST-NAME (WS15-IDX) 
                 WITH POINTER           WS15-STR-PTR
             END-UNSTRING   

             ADD 1                   TO WS15-IDX

           END-PERFORM  

           PERFORM  
               UNTIL (WS15-IDX > WS10-NUM-PAX)  
             MOVE   SPACE   
                 TO WS11-LAST-NAME (WS15-IDX)   
             ADD 1                   TO WS15-IDX
           END-PERFORM

                                     TO WS15-GENERIC-NUMERIC
               MOVE   WS15-GENERIC-NUMERIC  
                   TO WS11-AGE (WS15-IDX)   

               IF      (WS11-AGE (WS15-IDX) 
                        = WS15-GENERIC-NUMERIC) 
                 CONTINUE   
               ELSE 

      *          Unexpected sign or decimal fraction.   

                 PERFORM C8808-INVLD-AGE
               END-IF   
             ELSE   
               PERFORM C8808-INVLD-AGE  
             END-IF 

             ADD 1                   TO WS15-IDX

           END-PERFORM  

           PERFORM  
               UNTIL (WS15-IDX > WS10-NUM-PAX)  
             MOVE   0   
                 TO WS11-AGE (WS15-IDX) 
             ADD 1                   TO WS15-IDX
           END-PERFORM  

      *    ------------

PPC470*            END-IF   
PPC470             EXEC SQL 
PPC470                SELECT ABM.PBS_UPGRADE_TYPE   
PPC470                  INTO :WS-PBS-UPGRADE-TYPE   
PPC470                  FROM SAIL_ADD_BNFT      SAB 
PPC470                  JOIN ADD_BENEFIT_MASTER ABM 
                          ON ABM.PRP_OWNER_CODE   = SAB.PRP_OWNER_CODE  
                         AND ABM.PRP_OWNER_ID     = SAB.PRP_OWNER_ID
                       WHERE SAB.SHIP_CODE        = :TDP01-SHIP-CODE
                         AND SAB.SAIL_ID          = :TDP01-SAIL-ID  
                         AND SAB.ADD_BENEFIT_CODE = 
                                    :RSP06-I-TOMR-AB-CODE (WS-TOAR-SUB) 
                         AND SAB.ADD_BENEFIT_TYPE = 
                                    :RSP06-I-TOMR-AB-TYPE (WS-TOAR-SUB) 
                   END-EXEC 
                   IF SQLCODE < 0   
                      GO TO 9150-RDMS-ERROR 
                   ELSE 
                      IF SQLCODE = 100  
                         PERFORM C8855-SPIUR-NOT-FOUND

PRCCMB*                 UNTIL WS-OFF-COMP > WS-OVR-ENT-CNT(WS-OFF-COMP) 
PRCCMB* 
PRCCMB*             PERFORM VARYING WS-COMP-SUB FROM 1 BY 1 
PRCCMB*                     UNTIL WS-COMP-SUB > RSP06-ENTITY-CNT
PRCCMB*                                               (WS-GST-SUB)  OR  
PRCCMB*                (WS-OVR-ENTITY-TYPE (WS-OFF-SUB WS-OFF-COMP) =   
PRCCMB*                       RSP06-ENTITY-TYPE (WS-GST-SUB WS-COMP-SUB)
PRCCMB*                 AND WS-OVR-ENTITY-CODE(WS-OFF-SUB WS-OFF-COMP) =
PRCCMB*                     RSP06-ENTITY-CODE (WS-GST-SUB WS-COMP-SUB)) 
PRCCMB*                 CONTINUE
PRCCMB*             END-PERFORM 
PRCCMB* 
PRCCMB*             IF WS-COMP-SUB > RSP06-ENTITY-CNT (WS-GST-SUB)  
PRCCMB* 
PRCCMB*                MOVE WS-OVR-ENTITY-TYPE  
PRCCMB*                 (WS-OFF-SUB WS-OFF-COMP) TO 
PRCCMB*                 RSP06-ENTITY-TYPE (WS-GST-SUB WS-COMP-SUB)  
PRCCMB*                MOVE WS-OVR-ENTITY-CODE  
PRCCMB*                 (WS-OFF-SUB WS-OFF-COMP) TO 
PRCCMB*                 RSP06-ENTITY-CODE (WS-GST-SUB WS-COMP-SUB)  
PRCCMB*                MOVE WS-OVR-POS-1-OBC-AMT
PRCCMB*                (WS-OFF-SUB WS-OFF-COMP) TO  
PRCCMB*                 RSP06-POS-1-OBC-AMT (WS-GST-SUB WS-COMP-SUB)
PRCCMB*                MOVE WS-OVR-POS-2-OBC-AMT
PRCCMB*                (WS-OFF-SUB WS-OFF-COMP) TO  
PRCCMB*                 RSP06-POS-2-OBC-AMT (WS-GST-SUB WS-COMP-SUB)
PRCCMB*                MOVE WS-OVR-POS-3-OBC-AMT
PRCCMB*                (WS-OFF-SUB WS-OFF-COMP) TO  
PRCCMB*                  RSP06-POS-3-OBC-AMT (WS-GST-SUB WS-COMP-SUB)   
PRCCMB*                MOVE WS-OVR-POS-4-OBC-AMT
PRCCMB*                (WS-OFF-SUB WS-OFF-COMP) TO  
PRCCMB*                 RSP06-POS-4-OBC-AMT (WS-GST-SUB WS-COMP-SUB)
PRCCMB*                MOVE WS-OVR-POS-5-OBC-AMT
PRCCMB*                (WS-OFF-SUB WS-OFF-COMP) TO  
PRCCMB*                  RSP06-POS-5-OBC-AMT (WS-GST-SUB WS-COMP-SUB)   
PRCCMB*                MOVE WS-OVR-POS-1-ADJUSTMENT-AMT 
PRCCMB*                (WS-OFF-SUB WS-OFF-COMP) TO  
PRCCMB*                               RSP06-POS-1-ADJUSTMENT-AMT
PRCCMB*                              (WS-GST-SUB WS-COMP-SUB)

PRCCMB*                (WS-OFF-SUB WS-OFF-COMP) TO  
PRCCMB*                               RSP06-POS-2-ADJUSTMENT-AMT
PRCCMB*                              (WS-GST-SUB WS-COMP-SUB)   
PRCCMB*                 MOVE WS-OVR-POS-3-ADJUSTMENT-AMT
PRCCMB*                (WS-OFF-SUB WS-OFF-COMP) TO  
PRCCMB*                               RSP06-POS-3-ADJUSTMENT-AMT
PRCCMB*                              (WS-GST-SUB WS-COMP-SUB)   
PRCCMB*                 MOVE WS-OVR-POS-4-ADJUSTMENT-AMT
PRCCMB*                (WS-OFF-SUB WS-OFF-COMP) TO

PRCCMB* 
PRCCMB*             END-IF

       2110-ADJUST-CONTACT-LEVELS.  

      *    ------------ 

      *    Before we move guest entries back to we check if any 
      *    have contact data referring to the group level.  This
      *    will affect how we represent cabin level contact data as 
      *    we later move it into each pax.  

      *    We don't support some guests in cabin referring to group 
      *    contact data while others refer to cabin level. If any   
      *    are group, then all cabin level contact data will be 
      *    converted to pax level.  

           PERFORM  
               VARYING  WS11-PAX-IDX
                   FROM 1 BY 1  
               UNTIL   (WS11-PAX-IDX > WS10-NUM-PAX)
                   OR  (  WS10-CONTACT-ADDR-IND (WS11-PAX-IDX)  
                        = 'G')  
             CONTINUE   
           END-PERFORM. 

           IF      (WS11-PAX-IDX > WS10-NUM-PAX)
      *      No adjustment needed if no group level.

             CONTINUE   
           ELSE 
      *      Convert any cabin level contact information provided   
      *      with any guest to pax level.   

             PERFORM
                 VARYING  WS11-PAX-IDX  
                     FROM 1 BY 1
                 UNTIL   (WS11-PAX-IDX > WS10-NUM-PAX)  

               IF      (  WS10-CONTACT-ADDR-IND (WS11-PAX-IDX)  
                        = 'C')  

                 MOVE   'P' 
                     TO WS10-CONTACT-ADDR-IND (WS11-PAX-IDX)

      *          It is assumed that the client is already repeating 
      *          the the same cabin level data for each guest marked
      *          as having cabin level data.  So that doesn't have to   
      *          be done here.  
               END-IF   
             END-PERFORM
           END-IF.  

      *    ------------ 

       2110-X.  
           EXIT.

      *-----------------------------------------------------------------

       2130-MOVE-GUEST-DATA.

      *    Since the list of guests in this cabin are to be determined  
      *    solely on what is input, we move existing guest from RSP06   
      *    to a holding area.  RSP06 will then be set to have no
      *    guests.  
      * 
      *    Each guest provided on input with a non-zero sequence number 
      *    will be searched for in the hold area.  If a match is found, 
      *    the guest data from hold area will be readded to RSP06.  
      *    Whatever input data is provided for the guest will then  
      *    be moved to RSP06 to overlay existing data.  If an input 
      *    guest can not be found in hold area, an error exists.
      * 
      *    New guests will be indicated with a sequence number of zero. 
      *    An RSP06 entry will be initialized for each and input fields 
      * 
      *    Cancelled guests will be omitted on input.  Their data will  
      *    never be moved from the hold area back to RSP06. 

      *    Guest statistics in WS05 will be counted along the way.  
            MOVE RSP06-PAX-CNT  
                            TO WS05-OLD-PAX-CNT 

114988     IF (WS10-ACTION-IND = 'm' OR 'a') AND
114988        (WS10-NUM-PAX    > WS05-OLD-PAX-CNT)  
114988        PERFORM VARYING SUB FROM 1 BY 1   
114988           UNTIL SUB > WS10-NUM-PAX   
114988           IF SUB > WS05-OLD-PAX-CNT AND  
114988              WS11-SEQ-NBR (SUB) = 0  
114988              MOVE SPACES  TO WS11-PRE-PKG-CODE  (SUB)
114988                              WS11-PRE-PKG-NUM   (SUB)
114988                              WS11-PST-PKG-CODE  (SUB)
114988                              WS11-PST-PKG-NUM   (SUB)
114988                              WS11-IN-ARRIVAL-CITY-CODE (SUB) 
114988                              WS11-OUT-DEPART-CITY-CODE (SUB) 
114988              MOVE 0       TO WS11-PRE-NUM-CARS  (SUB)
114988                              WS11-PST-NUM-CARS  (SUB)
114988                              WS11-PRE-NUM-XFERS (SUB)
114988                              WS11-PST-NUM-XFERS (SUB)
114988           END-IF 
114988        END-PERFORM   
114988     END-IF.  
           MOVE 0                    TO WS05-ADDED-PAX-CNT. 
           MOVE 0                    TO WS05-MODIFIED-PAX-CNT.  

      *    First move existing guest data to hold area.

      *      Client is aware of all the yes-insurance indicators,   
      *      'S' or 'G' that may be on a pax.  For a no-insurance   
      *      indicator, it is aware only of 'N' or space.  Others may   
      *      exist though that have no-insurance included with other
      *      meanings.  
      * 
      *      So existing yes-insurance indicator ('S' or 'G') can be
      *      overlayed with whatever comes from client (its up to RSO25 
      *      or RSO26 to validate).  Existing no-insurance indicator
      *      ('N' or other unspecified options) can only be overlayed   
      *      with a yes-insurance indicator ('S' or 'G').   

           IF     (   RSP06-PAX-INSURANCE-IND (WS11-PAX-IDX)
                   <= SPACE)
               OR (  RSP06-PAX-INSURANCE-IND (WS11-PAX-IDX) 
                   = 'S')   
               OR (  RSP06-PAX-INSURANCE-IND (WS11-PAX-IDX) 
                   = 'G')   
               OR (  RSP06-PAX-INSURANCE-IND (WS11-PAX-IDX) 
                   = 'Y')   

             MOVE   WS11-INSURANCE-IND (WS11-PAX-IDX)   
                 TO RSP06-PAX-INSURANCE-IND (RSP06-PAX-CNT) 
           ELSE 

      *      An existing no-insurance indicator can be overlayed
      *      only by a yes-insurance indicator. 

             IF      (  WS11-INSURANCE-IND (WS11-PAX-IDX)   
                      = 'S')
                 OR  (  WS11-INSURANCE-IND (WS11-PAX-IDX)   
                      = 'G')
                 OR  (  WS11-INSURANCE-IND (WS11-PAX-IDX)   
                      = 'Y')

               MOVE   WS11-INSURANCE-IND (WS11-PAX-IDX) 
                   TO RSP06-PAX-INSURANCE-IND (RSP06-PAX-CNT)   
             END-IF 

           END-IF

       3800-MOVE-CONTACT-INPUT. 

           IF    (  WS10-CONTACT-ADDR-IND (WS11-PAX-IDX)
                  = 'X')
             OR  (WS10-CABIN-UPDATE-IND NOT = 'Y')  

      *      'X' means no change.  So, leave data fields and
      *      READ and EXIST indicators as initialized or as 
      *      set by RSO23.  
      * 
      *      Contact data is built on client as part of IBR data
      *      So, it cannot be loaded onto pax unless IBR data is
      *      also being loaded. 
      * 

             CONTINUE   
           ELSE 

168921*      MOVE   WS10-CONTACT-ADDR-IND (WS11-PAX-IDX)
168921*          TO RSP06-PAX-ANAR-CODE (RSP06-PAX-CNT) 

      *      The steps below require that, if this is not a new 
      *      berthing, that RSO23 would have been called to read
      *      all the given records before coming here.  Or, if  
      *      berthing, they all should have been initialized.   

103003*      MOVE   'Y' 
103003*          TO RSP06-PIAR-READ-IND (RSP06-PAX-CNT) 
103003*      MOVE   'Y' 
103003*          TO RSP06-PIER-READ-IND (RSP06-PAX-CNT)

168921*          TO RSP06-ANAR-READ-IND (RSP06-PAX-CNT)

168921*               = 'P')

103003*        MOVE   'Y'   
103003*            TO RSP06-PIAR-EXIST-IND (RSP06-PAX-CNT)  
103003*        MOVE   'Y'   
103003*            TO RSP06-PIER-EXIST-IND (RSP06-PAX-CNT)  
168921*        MOVE   'Y'   
168921*            TO RSP06-ANAR-EXIST-IND (RSP06-PAX-CNT)  
168921* 
168921*        MOVE   SPACE 
168921*            TO RSP06-ANAR-AGENCY-NAME (RSP06-PAX-CNT)
168921*        MOVE   SPACE 
168921*            TO RSP06-ANAR-GROUP-NAME (RSP06-PAX-CNT)

168921*            TO RSP06-ANAR-ADDRESS1 (RSP06-PAX-CNT)   
168921*        MOVE   WS10-CONTACT-ADDR-LINE2 (WS11-PAX-IDX)
168921*            TO RSP06-ANAR-ADDRESS2 (RSP06-PAX-CNT)

168921*            TO RSP06-ANAR-CITY (RSP06-PAX-CNT)

103003*            TO RSP06-PIER-EMAIL-ADDR (RSP06-PAX-CNT) 

      *        Nothing in PIAR gets overlayed.  It is left  
      *        initialized or as read from RSO23.   

168921*      ELSE   
      *        Any contact information for this guest will be   
      *        same as that for cabin or group.  So guest level

                             = 'Y') 
                  CONTINUE  
                ELSE
                  MOVE   'N'
                       TO RSP06-PIAR-EXIST-IND (RSP06-PAX-CNT)  
                END-IF  
168921*      END-IF

           CONTINUE.

114465 C8922-VALIDATE-AGENT.
114465  
114465*    Determine alternate customer number from input agent phone   
114465*    number.  
114465  
CCLAUS     EXEC SQL 
CCLAUS          SELECT COUNTRY_NUM  
CCLAUS            INTO :WS-COUNTRY-NUM  
CCLAUS            FROM VALID_COUNTRY
CCLAUS            WHERE COUNTRY_CODE = :WS10-PHONE-COUNTRY-CODE 
CCLAUS     END-EXEC.
CCLAUS  
CCLAUS     IF SQLCODE < 0   
CCLAUS        GO TO 9150-RDMS-ERROR 
CCLAUS     ELSE 
CCLAUS        IF SQLCODE = 100  
CCLAUS           GO TO C8915-MAIN-APR-CTRY-NOT-FND  
CCLAUS        END-IF
CCLAUS     END-IF.  
114465     MOVE WS10-AGENT-PHONE-NUM
114465                                 TO APR-PHONE-NUM.
CCLAUS     MOVE WS-COUNTRY-NUM         TO APR-COUNTRY-NUM.  
114465     PERFORM C8924-FETCH-APR THRU C8924-EXIT. 
114465     IF  WS01-APR-WAS-NOT-FETCHED 
114465         GO TO C8835-APR-NOT-FOUND
114465     END-IF.  
114465  
114465     PERFORM C8925-FETCH-AGENT THRU C8925-EXIT.   
114465     IF  WS01-AGENT-WAS-NOT-FETCHED   
114465         GO TO C8926-AGENT-NOT-FOUND  
114465     END-IF.  
114465     MOVE AGENT-CUSTOMER-NUM     TO WS23-CUSTOMER-NUM.
114465  
148695     IF AGENT-DIRECT-IND NOT = 'Y'
148695        MOVE 'N'    TO WS-AGENT-DIRECT-IND
148695     ELSE 
148695        MOVE 'Y'    TO WS-AGENT-DIRECT-IND
148695     END-IF.  
148695     IF RSP06-IBR-BOOK-NUM <= SPACES  
148695        MOVE 'N'    TO WS-AGENCY-TAKEOVER-IND 
148695     ELSE 
148695        IF WS-OLD-IBR-CUSTOMER-NUM = AGENT-CUSTOMER-NUM   
148695           MOVE 'N' TO WS-AGENCY-TAKEOVER-IND 
148695        ELSE  
148695           MOVE 'Y' TO WS-AGENCY-TAKEOVER-IND 
148695        END-IF
148695     END-IF.  
C85260     MOVE AGENT-PHONE-NUM        TO ATCR-PHONE-NUM
C85260     MOVE 'C'                    TO ATCR-COMPANY-CODE 
C85260     PERFORM C8928-FETCH-ATCR THRU C8928-EXIT.
C85260     IF  WS01-ATCR-WAS-NOT-FETCHED
C85260         GO TO C8874-ATCR-NOT-FOUND   
C85260     END-IF.  
C85260     MOVE ATCR-AGENCY-INDICATORS TO MKP40A-INDICATORS.
C85260  
114465 C8922-VALIDATE-AGENT-EXIT.   
114465     EXIT.
114465  
114465 C8923-VALIDATE-ALT-AGENT.
114465  
114465*    Determine alternate customer number from input alternate 
114465*    agent phone number.  
114465  
CCLAUS     EXEC SQL 
CCLAUS          SELECT COUNTRY_NUM  
CCLAUS            INTO :WS-ALT-COUNTRY-NUM  
CCLAUS            FROM VALID_COUNTRY
CCLAUS            WHERE COUNTRY_CODE = :WS10-ALT-PHONE-COUNTRY-CODE 
CCLAUS     END-EXEC.
CCLAUS  
CCLAUS     IF SQLCODE < 0   
CCLAUS        GO TO 9150-RDMS-ERROR 
CCLAUS     ELSE 
CCLAUS        IF SQLCODE = 100  
CCLAUS           GO TO C8915-ALT-APR-CTRY-NOT-FND   
CCLAUS        END-IF
CCLAUS     END-IF.  
114465     MOVE WS10-ALT-AGENT-PHONE-NUM
114465                                 TO APR-PHONE-NUM.
CCLAUS     MOVE WS-ALT-COUNTRY-NUM     TO APR-COUNTRY-NUM.  
114465     PERFORM C8924-FETCH-APR THRU C8924-EXIT. 
114465     IF  WS01-APR-WAS-NOT-FETCHED 
114465         GO TO C8836-ALT-APR-NOT-FOUND
114465     END-IF.  
114465  
114465     PERFORM C8925-FETCH-AGENT THRU C8925-EXIT.   
114465  
114465     IF WS01-AGENT-WAS-NOT-FETCHED
114465        GO TO C8927-ALT-AGENT-NOT-FOUND   
114465     END-IF.  
114465  
114465     MOVE AGENT-CUSTOMER-NUM     TO WS23-ALT-CUSTOMER-NUM.
114465  
148695     IF WS-AGENT-DIRECT-IND = 'Y' 
148695        IF AGENT-DIRECT-IND NOT = 'Y' 
148695           MOVE 'N' TO WS-AGENT-DIRECT-IND
148695        ELSE  
148695           MOVE 'Y' TO WS-AGENT-DIRECT-IND
148695        END-IF
148695     END-IF.  
148695     IF WS-AGENCY-TAKEOVER-IND NOT = 'Y'  
148695        IF RSP06-IBR-BOOK-NUM > SPACES
148695           MOVE 'N'    TO WS-AGENCY-TAKEOVER-IND  
148695        ELSE  
148695           IF WS-OLD-IBR-ALT-CUSTOMER-NUM = AGENT-CUSTOMER-NUM
148695              MOVE 'N' TO WS-AGENCY-TAKEOVER-IND  
148695           ELSE   
148695              MOVE 'Y' TO WS-AGENCY-TAKEOVER-IND  
148695           END-IF 
148695        END-IF
148695     END-IF.  

114465 C8923-VALIDATE-ALT-AGENT-EXIT.   
114465     EXIT.

119425 D0410-VALIDATE-TGO-OFFER.

194736     EXEC SQL DECLARE TARGET_OFFERS CURSOR FOR RETENTION  
194736              SELECT OFF.OFFER_ID, OFF.REDEMPTION_CNT,
PPC470*                    MST.MAX_REDEEM_CNT   
PPC470                     IFNULL(MST.MAX_REDEEM_CNT,99999) 
194736                FROM TARGET_OFFER OFF 
194736                JOIN TARGET_OFFER_MASTER MST  
194736                     ON OFF.OFFER_ID = MST.OFFER_ID   
PPC470*    WHERE (OFF.IDENTIFIER_TYPE = 'PG') AND   
PPC470     WHERE (OFF.IDENTIFIER_TYPE = 'PG') AND   
PPC470*            (OFF.IDENTIFIER =
PPC470*                    :RSP06-PAX-PAST-GUEST-NUM (WS-IX)) AND   
194736             (:RSP06-IBR-SAIL-DATE >= 
194736                 INT(CONVERT(SAIL_BEGIN_DATE, 112))) AND

PPC470*            (:WS01-SYS-DATE       >=

PPC470*            (:WS01-SYS-DATE       <= 
PPC470*                INT(CONVERT(BOOK_END_DATE, 112)))   AND  
PPC470             (:WS01-SYS-DATE-TIME       >=
PPC470                 INT(CONVERT(BOOK_BEGIN_DATETIME, 112))) AND  
PPC470             (:WS01-SYS-DATE-TIME       <=
PPC470                 INT(CONVERT(BOOK_END_DATETIME, 112)))   AND  
PPC470*            (:SPR1-PRICE-TYPE      = 
PPC470*                SUBSTR(RATE_CODE_1,1,1))            AND

194736     END-EXEC.

           IF SQLCODE < 0   
              GO TO 9150-RDMS-ERROR

119425 D0410-NEXT-PST-GUEST-OFFER.  
194736*    FETCH NEXT TARGET-OFFER-ASSOC-REC
194736*        WITHIN TARGET-PERSON-ASSOC-SET SET   
194736*       ON ERROR GO TO D0410-FETCH-TOPR-TOAR-ERROR.   
194736*    MOVE TOAR-OFFER-ID      TO TOMR-OFFER-ID.
194736*    MOVE TOAR-PERSON-ID     TO WS1-TOAR-PERSON-ID.   
194736*    MOVE 'N'                TO IRP03-FOUND-FLAG. 
194736*    IF TOAR-REDEEM-CNT > 0   
194736*       PERFORM VARYING IRP03-SEARCH-SUB FROM 1 BY 1  
194736*          UNTIL ((IRP03-SEARCH-SUB > TOAR-REDEEM-CNT) OR 
194736*                 (IRP03-FOUND-FLAG = 'Y'))   
194736*             IF RSP06-IBR-BOOK-NUM = 
194736*                TOAR-REDEEM-BOOK-NUM (IRP03-SEARCH-SUB)  
194736*                 MOVE 'Y'   TO IRP03-FOUND-FLAG  
194736*             END-IF  
194736*       END-PERFORM.  

194736*    FETCH OWNER RECORD WITHIN TARGET-MASTER-ASSOC-SET SET
194736*       ON ERROR GO TO D0410-NEXT-PST-GUEST-OFFER.

194736*    IF ((RSP06-IBR-SAIL-DATE >= TOMR-SAIL-EFF-DATE) AND  
194736*        (RSP06-IBR-SAIL-DATE <= TOMR-SAIL-END-DATE) AND  
194736*        (DPP02-SYSTEM-DATE   >= TOMR-BOOK-EFF-DATE) AND  
194736*        (DPP02-SYSTEM-DATE   <= TOMR-BOOK-END-DATE) AND  
194736*        (SPR1-PRICE-TYPE      = TOMR-PRICE-TYPE)    AND  
194736*        (SPR1-PRICE-CODE      = TOMR-PRICE-CODE)    AND  
194736*        (TOMR-STATUS-CODE     = 'O' OR ' ')) 
194736*         IF ((TOMR-MAX-REDEEM-CNT = 0) OR
194736*             (TOAR-REDEEM-CNT  < TOMR-MAX-REDEEM-CNT) OR 
194736*             (IRP03-FOUND-FLAG = 'Y'))   
194736*              MOVE 'Y' TO WS-TGO-OFFER-EXIST 
194736*              GO TO D0410-VALIDATE-TGO-EXIT. 

194736     EXEC SQL FETCH NEXT FROM TARGET_OFFERS   
194736         INTO :PG-OFFER-ID,   
194736              :PG-REDEEM-CNT, 
194736              :PG-MAX-REDEEM-CNT  
194736     END-EXEC.

           IF SQLCODE < 0   
              GO TO 9150-RDMS-ERROR 
           ELSE 
              IF SQLCODE = 100  
                 GO TO D0410-FETCH-TOPR-TOAR-ERROR  
              END-IF
           END-IF.  

194736     MOVE 0 TO WS-REDEEM-CNT. 

194736     EXEC SQL SELECT COUNT(*) INTO :WS-REDEEM-CNT 
194736                FROM TARGET_REDEMPTION
194736               WHERE (IDENTIFIER_TYPE = 'PG') AND 
PPC470*                    (IDENTIFIER =
PPC470*                        :RSP06-PAX-PAST-GUEST-NUM (WS-IX)) AND   
194736                     (OFFER_ID = :PG-OFFER-ID) AND
194736                     (BOOK_NUM = :RSP06-IBR-BOOK-NUM) 
194736     END-EXEC.

194736     IF WS-REDEEM-CNT > 0 
194736        MOVE 'Y'   TO IRP03-FOUND-FLAG.   

194736     IF (PG-MAX-REDEEM-CNT = 0) OR
194736        (PG-REDEEM-CNT < PG-MAX-REDEEM-CNT) OR
194736        (IRP03-FOUND-FLAG = 'Y')  
194736         MOVE 'Y' TO WS-TGO-OFFER-EXIST   
194736         GO TO D0410-CLOSE-CURSOR.

119425     GO TO D0410-NEXT-PST-GUEST-OFFER.

119425 D0410-FETCH-TOPR-TOAR-ERROR. 
194736*    IF ERROR-STATUS > '000000'   
194736*       IF ERROR-STATUS = '000307' OR '000313'
194736*          MOVE 'Y'              TO WS-END-TOAR-SET   
194736*       ELSE  
194736*          GO TO 9100-DATABASE-ERROR. 

194736     MOVE 'Y' TO WS-END-TOAR-SET. 

119425 D0410-CLOSE-CURSOR.  
194736     EXEC SQL CLOSE TARGET_OFFERS END-EXEC.   

           IF SQLCODE < 0   
              GO TO 9150-RDMS-ERROR 
           END-IF.  

119425 D0410-VALIDATE-TGO-EXIT. 
119425     EXIT.

       D8900-CHECK-FATAL-DMS-ERROR. 

      *    This paragraph can be called to check if the recent  
      *    DMS failure was a normal record not found or end of set  
      *    record, or area.  If not, the situation is probably serious  
      *    enough to halt program and roll back.

           IF      (ERROR-NUM = '0000') 
               OR  (ERROR-NUM = '0007') 
               OR  (ERROR-NUM = '0013') 
               OR  (ERROR-NUM = '0077') 

             CONTINUE   

           ELSE 

144449*      GO TO D9100-DMS-GENERAL-ERROR  
144449       GO TO 9100-DATABASE-ERROR  

           END-IF   

           CONTINUE.

       8100-INIT-FOR-PRELIM.
           MOVE 0       TO PSUB.
           MOVE ALL 'N' TO WS-PAX-INIT-INDS.

       8110-INIT-NEXT-PAX.  
           ADD 1 TO PSUB.   

           IF PSUB > RSP06-PAX-CNT  
              GO TO 8149-EXIT.  

PPC470*    IF RSP06-PAX-FIRST-NAME (PSUB) <= SPACES 
PPC470*       MOVE 'Y' TO WS-INIT-FIRST-NAME-IND (PSUB) 
PPC470*                    WS-PAX-INIT-IND (PSUB)   
PPC470*       EVALUATE PSUB 
PPC470*          WHEN 1 
PPC470*             MOVE 'ONE' TO RSP06-PAX-FIRST-NAME (PSUB)   
PPC470*          WHEN 2 
PPC470*             MOVE 'TWO' TO RSP06-PAX-FIRST-NAME (PSUB)   
PPC470*          WHEN 3 
PPC470*             MOVE 'THREE' TO RSP06-PAX-FIRST-NAME (PSUB) 
PPC470*          WHEN 4 
PPC470*             MOVE 'FOUR' TO RSP06-PAX-FIRST-NAME (PSUB)  
PPC470*          WHEN 5 
PPC470*             MOVE 'FIVE' TO RSP06-PAX-FIRST-NAME (PSUB)  
PPC470*       END-EVALUATE. 

RE0711     IF RSP06-PAX-LAST-NAME (PSUB) <= SPACES  
RE0711        MOVE 'Y'     TO WS-INIT-LAST-NAME-IND (PSUB)  
RE0711                        WS-PAX-INIT-IND (PSUB)
RE0711        MOVE 'GUEST' TO RSP06-PAX-LAST-NAME (PSUB).   

RE0711     IF RSP06-PAX-SEX (PSUB) <= SPACES
RE0711        MOVE 'Y' TO WS-INIT-SEX-IND (PSUB)
RE0711                    WS-PAX-INIT-IND (PSUB)
RE0711        MOVE 'F' TO RSP06-PAX-SEX (PSUB). 

RE0711     IF RSP06-PAX-TITLE (PSUB) <= SPACES  
RE0711        MOVE 'Y' TO WS-INIT-TITLE-IND (PSUB)  
RE0711                    WS-PAX-INIT-IND (PSUB)
RE0711        EVALUATE RSP06-PAX-SEX (PSUB) 
RE0711           WHEN 'F'   
RE0711                MOVE 'MS'   TO RSP06-PAX-TITLE (PSUB) 
RE0711           WHEN 'M'   
RE0711                MOVE 'MR'   TO RSP06-PAX-TITLE (PSUB) 
RE0711           WHEN 'C'   
RE0711                MOVE 'MISS' TO RSP06-PAX-TITLE (PSUB) 
RE0711           WHEN 'I'   
RE0711                MOVE 'INFT' TO RSP06-PAX-TITLE (PSUB) 
RE0711        END-EVALUATE. 

RE0711     IF RSP06-PAX-AGE (PSUB) <= ZERO AND  
RE0711           RSP06-PAX-BIRTH-DATE (PSUB) <= ZERO
RE0711        MOVE 'Y' TO WS-INIT-AGE-IND (PSUB)
RE0711                    WS-PAX-INIT-IND (PSUB)
RE0711                    RSP06-PAX-AGE-CODE (PSUB) 
RE0711        IF RSP06-PAX-TITLE (PSUB) = 'I'   
RE0711           MOVE 1 TO RSP06-PAX-AGE (PSUB) 
RE0711        ELSE  
RE0711           IF RSP06-PAX-TITLE (PSUB) = 'C'
RE0711              MOVE 5 TO RSP06-PAX-AGE (PSUB)  
RE0711           ELSE   
RE0711              MOVE 30  TO RSP06-PAX-AGE (PSUB).   

RE0711     IF RSP06-PAX-BIRTH-DATE (PSUB) <= ZERO   
RE0711        MOVE RSP06-IBR-SAIL-DATE TO WS-SAIL-DATE  
RE0711        MOVE 'Y'                 TO WS-INIT-BIRTH-DATE-IND (PSUB) 
RE0711                                    WS-PAX-INIT-IND (PSUB)
RE0711        IF RSP06-PAX-AGE-CODE (PSUB) = 'M'
RE0711           SUBTRACT 1 FROM WS-SAIL-YEAR   
RE0711        ELSE  
RE0711           SUBTRACT RSP06-PAX-AGE (PSUB) FROM WS-SAIL-YEAR
RE0711        END-IF
RE0711        IF WS-SAIL-MONTH = 2 AND WS-SAIL-DAY = 29 
RE0711           MOVE 28 TO WS-SAIL-DAY 
RE0711        END-IF
RE0711        MOVE WS-SAIL-DATE TO RSP06-PAX-BIRTH-DATE (PSUB). 

PPC470*    IF WS-PAX-INIT-IND (PSUB) = 'Y'  
PPC470*       MOVE GBR1-DAMAGE-DEPOSIT-IND TO   
PPC470*            RSP06-PAX-DAMAGE-DEPOSIT-IND (PSUB). 

108267     IF RSP06-PAX-NATIONALITY-CODE (PSUB) <= SPACES   
108267        MOVE 'Y'                 TO WS-INIT-NATIONALITY-IND (PSUB)
108267        MOVE WS10-AGENT-COUNTRY  TO RSP06-PAX-NATIONALITY-CODE
108267                                    (PSUB)
108267     END-IF.  

           GO TO 8110-INIT-NEXT-PAX.

       8149-EXIT.   
           EXIT.

       8150-CLEAR-FOR-PRELIM.   
           MOVE 0       TO PSUB.

       8160-CLEAR-NEXT-PAX. 
           ADD 1 TO PSUB.   

           IF PSUB > RSP06-PAX-CNT  
              GO TO 8199-EXIT.  

           IF WS-PAX-INIT-IND (PSUB) = 'N'  
              GO TO 8160-CLEAR-NEXT-PAX.

           IF WS-INIT-LAST-NAME-IND (PSUB) = 'Y'
              MOVE SPACES TO RSP06-PAX-LAST-NAME (PSUB).

PPC470*    IF WS-INIT-FIRST-NAME-IND (PSUB) = 'Y'   
PPC470*       MOVE SPACES TO RSP06-PAX-FIRST-NAME (PSUB).   

           IF WS-INIT-TITLE-IND (PSUB) = 'Y'
              MOVE SPACES TO RSP06-PAX-TITLE (PSUB).

           IF WS-INIT-SEX-IND (PSUB) = 'Y'  
              MOVE SPACES TO RSP06-PAX-SEX (PSUB).  

           IF WS-INIT-AGE-IND (PSUB) = 'Y'  
              MOVE SPACE TO RSP06-PAX-AGE-CODE (PSUB)   
              MOVE ZERO  TO RSP06-PAX-AGE (PSUB).   

           IF WS-INIT-BIRTH-DATE-IND (PSUB) = 'Y'   
              MOVE ZERO   TO RSP06-PAX-BIRTH-DATE (PSUB).   

108267     IF WS-INIT-NATIONALITY-IND (PSUB) = 'Y'  
108267        MOVE SPACES TO RSP06-PAX-NATIONALITY-CODE (PSUB)  
PPC470*       MOVE 'N'    TO RSP06-PAX-RESIDENCY-IND    (PSUB)  
108267     END-IF.  

           GO TO 8160-CLEAR-NEXT-PAX.   

       8199-EXIT.

148695                 MOVE 1003 TO DPP42-HOLD-MSG-INDEX
148695                 GO TO 9200-EDIT-ERROR-MSG
148695              END-IF  
148695           END-IF 
148695        END-IF
148695     END-IF.  

148695     MOVE WS11-HOME-PHONE-NUM (WS11-PAX-IDX)  
148695                                    TO WS-HOLD-PPHOR-PHONE-LST16. 

148695     IF WS-HOME-SUB <= ZEROES 
148695        IF WS-HOLD-PPHOR-PHONE-LST16 > ZEROES 
148695           ADD 1 TO RSP06-PPHOR-PHONE-CNT (RSP06-PAX-CNT) 
148695           MOVE RSP06-PPHOR-PHONE-CNT (RSP06-PAX-CNT) 
148695                                  TO WS-PPHOR-PHONE-CNT   

148695           MOVE 'H'               TO RSP06-PPHOR-PHONE-TYPE   
148695                                (RSP06-PAX-CNT WS-PPHOR-PHONE-CNT)

148695           MOVE WS-HOLD-PPHOR-PHONE-NUM TO RSP06-PPHOR-PHONE-NUM  
148695                                (RSP06-PAX-CNT WS-PPHOR-PHONE-CNT)

148695*          MOVE 'N'               TO RSP06-PPHOR-SEND-TXT-MSG-IND 
148695*                               (RSP06-PAX-CNT WS-PPHOR-PHONE-CNT)

148695           MOVE SPACES           TO RSP06-PPHOR-SEND-TXT-PREF-IND 
148695                                (RSP06-PAX-CNT WS-PPHOR-PHONE-CNT)
148695                                    RSP06-PPHOR-SEND-TXT-MSG-IND  
148695                                (RSP06-PAX-CNT WS-PPHOR-PHONE-CNT)

148695           MOVE ZEROES           TO RSP06-PPHOR-SAIL-TXT-OPT-FLG  
148695                                (RSP06-PAX-CNT WS-PPHOR-PHONE-CNT)
148695                                    RSP06-PPHOR-PAYM-TXT-OPT-FLG  
148695                                (RSP06-PAX-CNT WS-PPHOR-PHONE-CNT)
148695        END-IF
148695     ELSE 
148695        IF WS-HOLD-PPHOR-PHONE-LST16 <= ZEROES
148695           IF WS-HOME-SUB = RSP06-PPHOR-PHONE-CNT(RSP06-PAX-CNT)  
148695             SUBTRACT 1 FROM RSP06-PPHOR-PHONE-CNT (RSP06-PAX-CNT)
148695           ELSE   
148695             MOVE RSP06-PPHOR-PHONE-CNT (RSP06-PAX-CNT) TO
148695                              WS-PPHOR-PHONE-CNT  
148695             MOVE RSP06-PPHOR-PHONE-TBL   
148695                   (RSP06-PAX-CNT WS-PPHOR-PHONE-CNT) TO  
148695                  RSP06-PPHOR-PHONE-TBL   
148695                   (RSP06-PAX-CNT WS-HOME-SUB)
148695             SUBTRACT 1 FROM RSP06-PPHOR-PHONE-CNT (RSP06-PAX-CNT)
148695           END-IF 
148695        ELSE  
148695           MOVE WS-HOLD-PPHOR-PHONE-NUM TO RSP06-PPHOR-PHONE-NUM  
148695                            (RSP06-PAX-CNT WS-HOME-SUB)   
148695        END-IF
148695     END-IF.  

148695 8200-CHECK-PPHOR-EXIST-EXIT. 
148695     EXIT.

148695 8500-VALIDATE-PREF-IND.  
CCLAUS*    IF (WS-HOLD-PPHOR-PHONE-NUM (1:6) NOT =  
CCLAUS*                                         '000000' AND '000001')  
CCLAUS*       OR (WS-HOLD-PPHOR-PHONE-NUM (1:7) = '0000000')
CCLAUS     MOVE 'N'          TO WS-ALLOW-TEXT-PREF-IND. 
CCLAUS     IF WS11-RESIDENCE-COUNTRY (WS11-PAX-IDX) > SPACES
CCLAUS        IF WS11-RESIDENCE-COUNTRY (WS11-PAX-IDX) = 'US' OR 'CA'   
CCLAUS           MOVE 'Y'    TO WS-ALLOW-TEXT-PREF-IND  
CCLAUS        END-IF
CCLAUS     ELSE 
CCLAUS        IF WS11-NATIONALITY-CODE (WS11-PAX-IDX) = 'US' OR 'CA'
CCLAUS           MOVE 'Y'    TO WS-ALLOW-TEXT-PREF-IND  
CCLAUS        END-IF
CCLAUS     END-IF.  
CCLAUS     IF WS-ALLOW-TEXT-PREF-IND = 'N'  
148695        IF WS11-SEND-TEXT-PREF-IND (WS11-PAX-IDX) > SPACES
148695           MOVE SPACES TO WS11-SEND-TEXT-PREF-IND (WS11-PAX-IDX)  
148695        END-IF

148695        END-IF
148695        IF WS11-PAYM-TEXT-OPT-FLAG (WS11-PAX-IDX) = 'Y'   
148695           MOVE 'N' TO WS11-PAYM-TEXT-OPT-FLAG (WS11-PAX-IDX) 
148695        END-IF
148695     ELSE 
148695        IF WS11-SEND-TEXT-PREF-IND (WS11-PAX-IDX) <= SPACES

148695           MOVE 1007 TO DPP42-HOLD-MSG-INDEX  
148695           GO TO 9200-EDIT-ERROR-MSG  
148695        END-IF
148695        IF WS11-SAIL-TEXT-OPT-FLAG (WS11-PAX-IDX) <= SPACES   
148695           MOVE WS01-PROGRAM-NAME TO DPP42-HOLD-MSG-SOURCE
148695           MOVE 1008 TO DPP42-HOLD-MSG-INDEX  
148695           GO TO 9200-EDIT-ERROR-MSG  
148695        END-IF
148695        IF WS11-PAYM-TEXT-OPT-FLAG (WS11-PAX-IDX) <= SPACES   
148695           MOVE WS01-PROGRAM-NAME TO DPP42-HOLD-MSG-SOURCE
148695           MOVE 1009 TO DPP42-HOLD-MSG-INDEX

148695                 MOVE 1011 TO DPP42-HOLD-MSG-INDEX
148695                 GO TO 9200-EDIT-ERROR-MSG
148695              END-IF

148695     END-IF.

148695     PERFORM VARYING WS-PAX-SUB FROM 1 BY 1   
148695             UNTIL WS-PAX-SUB > RSP06-PAX-CNT 
148695         IF RSP06-PPHOR-EXIST-IND (WS-PAX-SUB) = 'Y'

148695                    UNTIL WS-SUB >
148695                                RSP06-PPHOR-PHONE-CNT (WS-PAX-SUB)
148695               IF RSP06-PPHOR-PHONE-TYPE (WS-PAX-SUB WS-SUB) = 'C'

148695                               TO WS-PAX-PREF-IND (WS-PAX-MOB-CNT)
148695               END-IF 
148695            END-PERFORM   
148695         END-IF   
148695     END-PERFORM.

148695                               TO WS-OLD-IBR-CUSTOMER-NUM.

148695 8600-BUILT-PAX-MOB-TBL-EXIT. 
148695     EXIT.

148695 8700-FIND-CHG-IN-MOB.

148695     MOVE 0                    TO WS-CUR-SUB. 

148695 8740-NEXT-CURR-PAX.  

148695     ADD 1                     TO WS-CUR-SUB. 

148695     IF WS-CUR-SUB > RSP06-PAX-CNT
148695        GO TO 8700-FIND-CHG-IN-MOB-EXIT   
148695     END-IF.  

148695     IF RSP06-PPHOR-EXIST-IND (WS-CUR-SUB) = 'N'  
148695        GO TO 8740-NEXT-CURR-PAX  
148695     END-IF.  

148695     PERFORM VARYING WS-CUR-PH-SUB FROM 1 BY 1
148695             UNTIL WS-CUR-PH-SUB >
148695                                RSP06-PPHOR-PHONE-CNT (WS-CUR-SUB)
148695             OR RSP06-PPHOR-PHONE-TYPE
148695                                  (WS-CUR-SUB WS-CUR-PH-SUB) = 'C'
148695        CONTINUE  
148695     END-PERFORM. 

148695     IF WS-CUR-PH-SUB > RSP06-PPHOR-PHONE-CNT (WS-CUR-SUB)
148695        GO TO 8740-NEXT-CURR-PAX  
148695     END-IF.  

148695     MOVE 0                    TO WS-NEW-SUB. 

148695 8750-NEXT-NEW-PAX.   

148695     ADD 1                     TO WS-NEW-SUB. 

148695     IF WS-NEW-SUB > RSP06-PAX-CNT
148695        GO TO 8760-UPDATE-PREF-IND
148695     END-IF.  

148695     IF RSP06-PPHOR-EXIST-IND (WS-NEW-SUB) = 'N'  
148695        GO TO 8750-NEXT-NEW-PAX   
148695     END-IF.  

148695     IF WS-NEW-SUB = WS-CUR-SUB   
148695        GO TO 8750-NEXT-NEW-PAX   
148695     END-IF.  

148695     PERFORM VARYING WS-NEW-PH-SUB FROM 1 BY 1
148695             UNTIL WS-NEW-PH-SUB >
148695                                RSP06-PPHOR-PHONE-CNT (WS-NEW-SUB)
148695             OR RSP06-PPHOR-PHONE-TYPE
148695                                  (WS-NEW-SUB WS-NEW-PH-SUB) = 'C'
148695        CONTINUE  
148695     END-PERFORM. 

148695     IF WS-NEW-PH-SUB > RSP06-PPHOR-PHONE-CNT (WS-NEW-SUB)
148695        GO TO 8750-NEXT-NEW-PAX   
148695     END-IF.  

148695     IF RSP06-PPHOR-PHONE-NUM (WS-CUR-SUB WS-CUR-PH-SUB) NOT =
148695        RSP06-PPHOR-PHONE-NUM (WS-NEW-SUB WS-NEW-PH-SUB)  
148695        IF RSP06-PPHOR-PHONE-NUM  
148695                            (WS-CUR-SUB WS-CUR-PH-SUB) (7:10) =   
148695           RSP06-PPHOR-PHONE-NUM (WS-NEW-SUB WS-NEW-PH-SUB) (7:10)
148695           AND (RSP06-PPHOR-PHONE-NUM (WS-CUR-SUB WS-CUR-PH-SUB)  
148695                                     (1:6) = '000000' OR '000001')
148695           AND (RSP06-PPHOR-PHONE-NUM (WS-NEW-SUB WS-NEW-PH-SUB)  
148695                                     (1:6) = '000000' OR '000001')
148695           CONTINUE   
148695        ELSE  
148695           GO TO 8750-NEXT-NEW-PAX
148695        END-IF
148695     END-IF.  

148695     IF RSP06-PPHOR-SEND-TXT-PREF-IND 
148695                              (WS-NEW-SUB WS-NEW-PH-SUB) NOT =
148695        RSP06-PPHOR-SEND-TXT-PREF-IND 
148695                              (WS-CUR-SUB WS-CUR-PH-SUB)  
148695        IF (RSP06-PPHOR-SEND-TXT-PREF-IND 
148695                     (WS-NEW-SUB WS-NEW-PH-SUB) NOT = 'Y' AND 'I')
148695           OR (RSP06-PPHOR-SEND-TXT-PREF-IND  
148695                     (WS-CUR-SUB WS-CUR-PH-SUB) NOT = 'Y' AND 'I')
148695           IF    (WS10-ACTION-IND = 'A')  
148695             OR  (WS10-ACTION-IND = 'M')  
148695             OR  (WS10-ACTION-IND = 'R')  
148695             OR  (WS10-ACTION-IND = 'P')  
148695             MOVE WS01-PROGRAM-NAME TO DPP42-HOLD-MSG-SOURCE  
148695             MOVE 1012 TO DPP42-HOLD-MSG-INDEX
148695             GO TO 9200-EDIT-ERROR-MSG
148695           END-IF 
148695        END-IF
148695     END-IF.  

148695     IF RSP06-PPHOR-SAIL-TXT-OPT-FLG  
148695                              (WS-NEW-SUB WS-NEW-PH-SUB) NOT =
148695        RSP06-PPHOR-SAIL-TXT-OPT-FLG  
148695                              (WS-CUR-SUB WS-CUR-PH-SUB) OR   
148695        RSP06-PPHOR-PAYM-TXT-OPT-FLG  
148695                              (WS-NEW-SUB WS-NEW-PH-SUB) NOT =
148695        RSP06-PPHOR-PAYM-TXT-OPT-FLG  
148695                              (WS-CUR-SUB WS-CUR-PH-SUB) OR   
148695        RSP06-PPHOR-SEND-TXT-MSG-IND  
148695                              (WS-NEW-SUB WS-NEW-PH-SUB) NOT =
148695        RSP06-PPHOR-SEND-TXT-MSG-IND  
148695                              (WS-CUR-SUB WS-CUR-PH-SUB)  
148695        IF    (WS10-ACTION-IND = 'A') 
148695          OR  (WS10-ACTION-IND = 'M') 
148695          OR  (WS10-ACTION-IND = 'R') 
148695          OR  (WS10-ACTION-IND = 'P') 
148695          MOVE WS01-PROGRAM-NAME TO DPP42-HOLD-MSG-SOURCE 
148695          MOVE 1012 TO DPP42-HOLD-MSG-INDEX   
148695          GO TO 9200-EDIT-ERROR-MSG   
148695        END-IF
148695     END-IF.  

148695     GO TO 8750-NEXT-NEW-PAX. 

148695 8760-UPDATE-PREF-IND.

148695     IF RSP06-PPHOR-SEND-TXT-PREF-IND (WS-CUR-SUB WS-CUR-PH-SUB)  
148695                                       NOT = 'N' AND > SPACES 
148695        PERFORM VARYING WS-SUB FROM 1 BY 1
148695                UNTIL WS-SUB > WS-PAX-MOB-CNT 
148695                OR WS-PAX-MOB-NUM (WS-SUB) = RSP06-PPHOR-PHONE-NUM
148695                                       (WS-CUR-SUB WS-CUR-PH-SUB) 
148695                OR (RSP06-PPHOR-PHONE-NUM 
148695                            (WS-CUR-SUB WS-CUR-PH-SUB) (7:10) =   
148695                    WS-PAX-MOB-NUM (WS-SUB) (7:10)
148695                    AND (RSP06-PPHOR-PHONE-NUM
148695                                        (WS-CUR-SUB WS-CUR-PH-SUB)
148695                                    (1:6) = '000000' OR '000001') 
148695                    AND (WS-PAX-MOB-NUM (WS-SUB)  
148695                                    (1:6) = '000000' OR '000001'))
148695           CONTINUE   
148695        END-PERFORM   
148695  
148695        IF WS-SUB > WS-PAX-MOB-CNT
148695           MOVE 'I'            TO RSP06-PPHOR-SEND-TXT-PREF-IND   
148695                                       (WS-CUR-SUB WS-CUR-PH-SUB) 
148695        ELSE  
148695           IF WS-PAX-PREF-IND (WS-SUB) = 'Y'  
148695              MOVE 'Y'         TO RSP06-PPHOR-SEND-TXT-PREF-IND   
148695                                       (WS-CUR-SUB WS-CUR-PH-SUB) 
148695           ELSE   
148695              MOVE 'I'         TO RSP06-PPHOR-SEND-TXT-PREF-IND   
148695                                       (WS-CUR-SUB WS-CUR-PH-SUB) 
148695           END-IF 
148695        END-IF
148695     END-IF.  

148695     GO TO 8740-NEXT-CURR-PAX.

148695 8700-FIND-CHG-IN-MOB-EXIT.   
148695     EXIT.

PPC470 7990-RT-APPLIED. 
PRCCMB*    EXEC SQL DECLARE COMP_COMM_INFO_CUR CURSOR   
PRCCMB*       SELECT SOCD.PRP_OWNER_CODE,SOCD.PRP_OWNER_ID, 
PRCCMB*              SOCD.CABIN_COMMISSION_AMT,SOCD.FULL_COMMISSION_PCT,
PRCCMB*              SOCD.FULL_COMMISSION_AMT,SOCD.EXTRA_COMMISSION_PCT,
PRCCMB*              SOCD.EXTRA_COMMISSION_AMT  
PRCCMB*             ,DECODE(SOCD.PRP_OWNER_CODE,'RTE','RTE','PCH','PCH',
PRCCMB*                DECODE(SOCD.PRP_OWNER_ID,OISI.EXTRA_AB_OWNER_ID, 
PRCCMB*                       'EAB',OISI.ADD_BNFT_OWNER_ID_1,'AB1', 
PRCCMB*                       OISI.ADD_BNFT_OWNER_ID_2,'AB2',   
PRCCMB*                       OISI.ADD_BNFT_OWNER_ID_2,'AB3',   
PRCCMB*                       OISI.ADD_BNFT_OWNER_ID_2,'AB4',   
PRCCMB*                       OISI.ADD_BNFT_OWNER_ID_2,'AB5',   
PRCCMB*                       OISI.ADD_BNFT_OWNER_ID_2,'AB6','OTH'))
PRCCMB*          FROM OFFER_ID_STATIC_INFO OISI,SAIL_OFFER_COMM_DTL SOCD
PRCCMB*          WHERE OISI.OFFER_ID = :WS-931-OFFER-ID 
PRCCMB*            AND SOCD.SHIP_CODE = :WS-931-SHIP-CODE   
PRCCMB*            AND SOCD.SAIL_ID   = :WS-931-SAIL-ID 
PRCCMB*            AND SOCD.OFFER_CODE = OISI.OFFER_CODE
PRCCMB*            AND SOCD.OFFER_ID = OISI.OFFER_ID
PRCCMB*            AND SOCD.CATEGORY_CODE = :WS-931-CPA-CAT-CODE
PRCCMB*    END-EXEC.
PRCCMB* 
PRCCMB*    EXEC SQL OPEN COMP_COMM_INFO_CUR END-EXEC.   
PRCCMB* 
PRCCMB*    IF SQLCODE < 0   
PRCCMB*       GO TO 9150-RDMS-ERROR.
PRCCMB* 
PRCCMB*    EXEC SQL FETCH NEXT 10 COMP_COMM_INFO_CUR
PRCCMB*         INTO WS-OWNER-CODE,WS-OWNER-ID  
PRCCMB*             ,WS-CMP-CAB-COMM-AMT
PRCCMB*             ,WS-CMP-FULL-COMM-AMT   
PRCCMB*             ,WS-CMP-FULL-COMM-PCT   
PRCCMB*             ,WS-CMP-XTRA-COMM-AMT   
PRCCMB*             ,WS-CMP-XTRA-COMM-PCT   
PRCCMB*             ,WS-CMP-COMPONENT-CODE  
PRCCMB*    END-EXEC.
PRCCMB* 
PRCCMB*    IF SQLCODE < 0   
PRCCMB*       GO TO 9150-RDMS-ERROR.
PRCCMB* 
PRCCMB*    MOVE AUX-INFO-REC-CNT TO WS-CMP-COMM-CNT.
PRCCMB* 
PRCCMB*    EXEC SQL CLOSE COMP_COMM_INFO_CUR END-EXEC.  
PRCCMB* 
PRCCMB*    IF SQLCODE < 0   
PRCCMB*       GO TO 9150-RDMS-ERROR.
PRCCMB* 
PRCCMB*    PERFORM VARYING SUB FROM 1 BY 1  
PRCCMB*            UNTIL SUB > WS-CMP-COMM-CNT  
PRCCMB*       EVALUATE WS-CMP-COMPONENT-CODE (SUB)  
PRCCMB*          WHEN 'RTE' MOVE WS-CMP-CAB-COMM-AMT (SUB) TO   
PRCCMB*                          WS-931-RTE-CABIN-COMM-AMT  
PRCCMB*                     MOVE WS-CMP-FULL-COMM-AMT (SUB) TO  
PRCCMB*                          WS-931-RTE-COMM-AMT
PRCCMB*                     MOVE WS-CMP-FULL-COMM-PCT (SUB) TO  
PRCCMB*                          WS-931-RTE-COMM-PCT
PRCCMB*          WHEN 'PCH' MOVE WS-CMP-CAB-COMM-AMT (SUB) TO   
PRCCMB*                          WS-931-PC-CABIN-COMM-AMT   
PRCCMB*                     MOVE WS-CMP-FULL-COMM-AMT (SUB) TO  
PRCCMB*                          WS-931-PC-COMM-AMT 
PRCCMB*                     MOVE WS-CMP-FULL-COMM-PCT (SUB) TO  
PRCCMB*                          WS-931-PC-COMM-PCT 
PRCCMB*          WHEN 'EAB' MOVE WS-CMP-FULL-COMM-AMT (SUB) TO  
PRCCMB*                          WS-931-EXTRA-COMM-AMT  
PRCCMB*                     MOVE WS-CMP-FULL-COMM-PCT (SUB) TO  
PRCCMB*                          WS-931-EXTRA-COMM-PCT  
PRCCMB*          WHEN 'AB1' MOVE WS-CMP-CAB-COMM-AMT (SUB) TO   
PRCCMB*                          WS-931-AB-CABIN-COMM-AMT-1 
PRCCMB*                     MOVE WS-CMP-FULL-COMM-AMT (SUB) TO  
PRCCMB*                          WS-931-AB-COMMISSION-AMT-1 
PRCCMB*                     MOVE WS-CMP-FULL-COMM-PCT (SUB) TO  
PRCCMB*                          WS-931-AB-COMMISSION-PCT-1 
PRCCMB*          WHEN 'AB2' MOVE WS-CMP-CAB-COMM-AMT (SUB) TO   
PRCCMB*                          WS-931-AB-CABIN-COMM-AMT-2 
PRCCMB*                     MOVE WS-CMP-FULL-COMM-AMT (SUB) TO  
PRCCMB*                          WS-931-AB-COMMISSION-AMT-2 
PRCCMB*                     MOVE WS-CMP-FULL-COMM-PCT (SUB) TO  
PRCCMB*                          WS-931-AB-COMMISSION-PCT-2 
PRCCMB*          WHEN 'AB3' MOVE WS-CMP-CAB-COMM-AMT (SUB) TO   
PRCCMB*                          WS-931-AB-CABIN-COMM-AMT-3 
PRCCMB*                     MOVE WS-CMP-FULL-COMM-AMT (SUB) TO  
PRCCMB*                          WS-931-AB-COMMISSION-AMT-3 
PRCCMB*                     MOVE WS-CMP-FULL-COMM-PCT (SUB) TO  
PRCCMB*                          WS-931-AB-COMMISSION-PCT-3 
PRCCMB*          WHEN 'AB4' MOVE WS-CMP-CAB-COMM-AMT (SUB) TO   
PRCCMB*                          WS-931-AB-CABIN-COMM-AMT-4 
PRCCMB*                     MOVE WS-CMP-FULL-COMM-AMT (SUB) TO  
PRCCMB*                          WS-931-AB-COMMISSION-AMT-1 
PRCCMB*                     MOVE WS-CMP-FULL-COMM-PCT (SUB) TO  
PRCCMB*                          WS-931-AB-COMMISSION-PCT-4 
PRCCMB*          WHEN 'AB5' MOVE WS-CMP-CAB-COMM-AMT (SUB) TO   
PRCCMB*                          WS-931-AB-CABIN-COMM-AMT-5 
PRCCMB*                     MOVE WS-CMP-FULL-COMM-AMT (SUB) TO  
PRCCMB*                          WS-931-AB-COMMISSION-AMT-5 
PRCCMB*                     MOVE WS-CMP-FULL-COMM-PCT (SUB) TO  
PRCCMB*                          WS-931-AB-COMMISSION-PCT-5 
PRCCMB*          WHEN 'AB6' MOVE WS-CMP-CAB-COMM-AMT (SUB) TO   
PRCCMB*                          WS-931-AB-CABIN-COMM-AMT-6 
PRCCMB*                     MOVE WS-CMP-FULL-COMM-AMT (SUB) TO  
PRCCMB*                          WS-931-AB-COMMISSION-AMT-6 
PRCCMB*                     MOVE WS-CMP-FULL-COMM-PCT (SUB) TO  
PRCCMB*                          WS-931-AB-COMMISSION-PCT-6 
PRCCMB*       END-EVALUATE  
PRCCMB*    END-PERFORM. 

PPC470     EXEC SQL 
PPC470       SELECT 
PRCCMB*          IFNULL(SID.RATE_BONUS_SAIL_DAYS_PCT, 0)
PRCCMB*          IFNULL(SID.PROCHC_BONUS_SAIL_DAYS_PCT, 0)  
PRCCMB*          IFNULL(SID.AB_BONUS_SAIL_DAYS_PCT_1, 0)
PRCCMB*          IFNULL(SID.AB_BONUS_SAIL_DAYS_PCT_2, 0)
PRCCMB*          IFNULL(SID.AB_BONUS_SAIL_DAYS_PCT_3, 0)
PRCCMB*          IFNULL(SID.AB_BONUS_SAIL_DAYS_PCT_4, 0)
PRCCMB*          IFNULL(SID.AB_BONUS_SAIL_DAYS_PCT_5, 0)
PRCCMB*          IFNULL(SID.AB_BONUS_SAIL_DAYS_PCT_6, 0)
PRCCMB*          ,IFNULL(SAL.GENERAL_TERM_COND_CODE,' ')
PRCCMB            IFNULL(SAL.GENERAL_TERM_COND_CODE,' ')
PPC470           ,SIDC.CABIN_COMMISSION_AMT

                 ,:WS-TERMS-LEVEL-CODE  
PPC470       FROM  SAILING                               SAL
PPC470       JOIN  SAIL_OFFER_ID                         SID
PPC470         ON (SID.SHIP_CODE = SAL.SHIP_CODE)   
PPC470        AND (SID.SAIL_ID =   SAL.SAIL_ID) 
PPC470        AND (SID.OFFER_ID =  :WS-931-OFFER-ID)
PPC470       JOIN  SAIL_OFFER_PRICE                      SIDC   
PPC470         ON (SIDC.SHIP_CODE = SID.SHIP_CODE)  
PPC470        AND (SIDC.SAIL_ID =   SID.SAIL_ID)
              AND (SIDC.ACTIVE_IND IN ('N','Y'))
              AND (SIDC.OFFER_CODE = SID.OFFER_CODE)
PPC470        AND (SIDC.OFFER_ID =  SID.OFFER_ID)   
PPC470        AND (SIDC.CPA_CATEGORY_CODE = :WS-931-CPA-CAT-CODE)   
PRCCMB*          LEFT JOIN COMMISSION_INFO RCOM 
PRCCMB*            ON RCOM.COMMISSION_INFO_ID = 
PRCCMB*               SIDC.RATE_COMMISSION_INFO_ID  
PRCCMB*          LEFT JOIN COMMISSION_INFO PCOM 
PRCCMB*            ON PCOM.COMMISSION_INFO_ID = 
PRCCMB*               SIDC.PROCHC_COMMISSION_INFO_ID
PRCCMB*          LEFT JOIN COMMISSION_INFO EACM 
PRCCMB*            ON EACM.COMMISSION_INFO_ID = 
PRCCMB*               SIDC.EXTRA_AB_COMMISSION_INFO_ID  
PRCCMB*          LEFT JOIN COMMISSION_INFO A1CM 
PRCCMB*            ON A1CM.COMMISSION_INFO_ID = 
PRCCMB*               SIDC.AB_COMMISSION_INFO_ID_1  
PRCCMB*          LEFT JOIN COMMISSION_INFO A2CM

PPC470         AND SAIL_ID   = :WS-931-SAIL-ID  
PPC470       FETCH FIRST 1 ROW ONLY 
PPC470     END-EXEC.
PPC470  
PPC470     IF SQLCODE < 0   
PPC470        GO TO 9150-RDMS-ERROR 
PPC470     ELSE 
PPC470        IF SQLCODE = 100  
PPC470           MOVE 8896 TO DPP42-HOLD-MSG-INDEX  
PPC470           GO TO 9200-EDIT-ERROR-MSG  
PPC470        END-IF
PPC470     END-IF.  
PPC470  
           EXEC SQL DECLARE SAIL_PMT_ITEM_CUR CURSOR
              SELECT ITEM_TYPE,ITEM_CODE
                 FROM SAIL_PAYMENT_ITEM 
                 WHERE SHIP_CODE = :WS-931-SHIP-CODE
                   AND SAIL_ID = :WS-931-SAIL-ID
                   AND PRP_OWNER_CODE = :U-931-OVR-PMT-OWNER-CODE   
                   AND PRP_OWNER_ID = :U-931-OVR-PMT-OWNER-ID   
                   AND TERM_LEVEL_CODE = :WS-TERMS-LEVEL-CODE   
                   AND REQUEST_CABIN_CODE IN ('~','B')  
                   AND LOCALITY_CODE IN ('~',:RSP06-IBR-LOCALITY-CODE)  
                   AND CURRENCY_CODE IN ('~',:RSP06-IBR-CURRENCY-CODE)  
                   AND (AGENT_CONSUMER_CODE = '~'   
                    OR  (:RSP06-IBR-DIRECT-IND = 'Y'
                   AND   AGENT_CONSUMER_CODE = 'C') 
                    OR  (:RSP06-IBR-DIRECT-IND = 'N'
                   AND   AGENT_CONSUMER_CODE = 'A'))
                   AND CONNECTION_CODE IN ('~',:RSP25-I-CONNECTION-CODE)
                 ORDER BY TERM_LEVEL_CODE,REQUEST_CABIN_CODE,   
                          LOCALITY_CODE,CURRENCY_CODE,  
                          AGENT_CONSUMER_CODE,CONNECTION_CODE   
           END-EXEC.

           EXEC SQL DECLARE SAIL_PEN_ITEM_CUR CURSOR
              SELECT ITEM_TYPE,ITEM_CODE
                 FROM SAIL_PENALTY_ITEM 
                 WHERE SHIP_CODE = :WS-931-SHIP-CODE
                   AND SAIL_ID = :WS-931-SAIL-ID
                   AND PRP_OWNER_CODE = :U-931-OVR-PEN-OWNER-CODE   
                   AND PRP_OWNER_ID = :U-931-OVR-PEN-OWNER-ID   
                   AND TERM_LEVEL_CODE = :WS-TERMS-LEVEL-CODE   
                   AND REQUEST_CABIN_CODE IN ('~','B')  
                   AND LOCALITY_CODE IN ('~',:RSP06-IBR-LOCALITY-CODE)  
                   AND CURRENCY_CODE IN ('~',   
                                         :RSP06-IBR-CURRENCY-CODE)  
                   AND (AGENT_CONSUMER_CODE = '~'   
                    OR  (:RSP06-IBR-DIRECT-IND = 'Y'
                   AND   AGENT_CONSUMER_CODE = 'C') 
                    OR  (:RSP06-IBR-DIRECT-IND = 'N'
                   AND   AGENT_CONSUMER_CODE = 'A'))
                   AND CONNECTION_CODE IN ('~',:RSP25-I-CONNECTION-CODE)
                 ORDER BY TERM_LEVEL_CODE,REQUEST_CABIN_CODE,   
                          LOCALITY_CODE,CURRENCY_CODE,  
                          AGENT_CONSUMER_CODE,CONNECTION_CODE   
           END-EXEC.

           MOVE SPACES TO WS-931-PMT-ITEM-CODE  
                          WS-931-PEN-ITEM-CODE. 

           IF U-931-OVR-PMT-OWNER-CODE > SPACES 
              EXEC SQL OPEN SAIL_PMT_ITEM_CUR END-EXEC  
              IF SQLCODE < ZERO 
                 GO TO 9150-RDMS-ERROR  
              END-IF
              EXEC SQL FETCH FIRST SAIL_PMT_ITEM_CUR
                 INTO WS-931-PMT-ITEM-TYPE, 
                      WS-931-PMT-ITEM-CODE  
              END-EXEC  
              IF SQLCODE < ZERO 
                 GO TO 9150-RDMS-ERROR  
              END-IF
              EXEC SQL CLOSE SAIL_PMT_ITEM_CUR END-EXEC 
              IF SQLCODE < ZERO 
                 GO TO 9150-RDMS-ERROR. 

           IF U-931-OVR-PEN-OWNER-CODE > SPACES 
              EXEC SQL OPEN SAIL_PEN_ITEM_CUR END-EXEC  
              IF SQLCODE < ZERO 
                 GO TO 9150-RDMS-ERROR  
              END-IF
              EXEC SQL FETCH FIRST SAIL_PEN_ITEM_CUR
                 INTO WS-931-PEN-ITEM-TYPE, 
                      WS-931-PEN-ITEM-CODE  
              END-EXEC  
              IF SQLCODE < ZERO 
                 GO TO 9150-RDMS-ERROR  
              END-IF
              EXEC SQL CLOSE SAIL_PEN_ITEM_CUR END-EXEC 
              IF SQLCODE < ZERO 
                 GO TO 9150-RDMS-ERROR. 

PPC470     IF WS-931-PMT-ITEM-CODE NOT > SPACES 
PPC470        EXEC SQL  
PPC470          SELECT SPI.ITEM_TYPE
PPC470                ,SPI.ITEM_CODE
PPC470            INTO :WS-931-PMT-ITEM-TYPE
PPC470                ,:WS-931-PMT-ITEM-CODE
PPC470            FROM  SAILING                          SAL
PPC470            JOIN  SAIL_PAYMENT_ITEM                SPI
PPC470              ON (SPI.SHIP_CODE = SAL.SHIP_CODE)  
PPC470             AND (SPI.SAIL_ID = SAL.SAIL_ID)  
PPC470             AND (SPI.PRP_OWNER_CODE = SAL.PRP_OWNER_CODE)
PPC470             AND (SPI.PRP_OWNER_ID = SAL.PRP_OWNER_ID)
                   AND TERM_LEVEL_CODE = :WS-TERMS-LEVEL-CODE   
PPC470             AND (SPI.REQUEST_CABIN_CODE IN ('~','B'))
PPC470             AND SPI.LOCALITY_CODE IN ('~',   
                                             :RSP50-O-LOCALITY-CODE)
PPC470             AND SPI.CURRENCY_CODE IN ('~',   
PPC470                                       :RSP06-IBR-CURRENCY-CODE)  
PPC470             AND ((SPI.AGENT_CONSUMER_CODE = '~') OR  
PPC470                  (SPI.AGENT_CONSUMER_CODE = 'C' AND  
PPC470                   :RSP06-IBR-DIRECT-IND = 'Y') OR
PPC470                  (SPI.AGENT_CONSUMER_CODE = 'A' AND  
PPC470                   :RSP06-IBR-DIRECT-IND = 'N'))  
PPC470             AND ((SPI.CONNECTION_CODE = '~')      OR 
PPC470                  (SPI.CONNECTION_CODE= :RSP50-O-CONNECTION-CODE))
PPC470           WHERE  SAL.SHIP_CODE = :WS-931-SHIP-CODE   
PPC470             AND  SAL.SAIL_ID   = :WS-931-SAIL-ID 
PPC470           FETCH FIRST 1 ROW ONLY 
PPC470        END-EXEC  
PPC470        IF SQLCODE < 0
PPC470           GO TO 9150-RDMS-ERROR  
PPC470        ELSE  
PPC470           IF SQLCODE = 100   
PPC470              MOVE 8905 TO DPP42-HOLD-MSG-INDEX   
PPC470              GO TO 9200-EDIT-ERROR-MSG   
PPC470           END-IF 
PPC470        END-IF
PPC470     END-IF.  
PPC470  
PPC470     EXEC SQL 
PPC470        SELECT IFNULL(REFUND_TERM_COND_CODE,'    ')   
PPC470              ,IFNULL(SCHEDULE_TERM_COND_CODE,'    ') 
PPC470          INTO :WS-931-REF-TERM-COND-CODE 
PPC470              ,:WS-931-SHED-TERM-COND-CODE
PPC470          FROM ITM_PAYMENT
PPC470         WHERE ITEM_TYPE = :WS-931-PMT-ITEM-TYPE  
PPC470           AND ITEM_CODE = :WS-931-PMT-ITEM-CODE  
PPC470         FETCH FIRST 1 ROW ONLY   
PPC470     END-EXEC.

PPC470     IF SQLCODE < 0   
PPC470        GO TO 9150-RDMS-ERROR 
PPC470     ELSE 
PPC470        IF SQLCODE = 100  
PPC470           MOVE 8899 TO DPP42-HOLD-MSG-INDEX  
PPC470           GO TO 9200-EDIT-ERROR-MSG  
PPC470        END-IF
PPC470     END-IF.  

PPC470     IF WS-931-REF-TERM-COND-CODE <= SPACES   
PPC470        MOVE U-931-OVR-OFF-REF-TERM-CODE  
PPC470                          TO WS-931-REF-TERM-COND-CODE
PPC470     END-IF.  

PPC470     IF WS-931-PEN-ITEM-CODE NOT > SPACES 
PPC470        EXEC SQL  
PPC470          SELECT  ITEM_CODE   
PPC470            INTO :WS-931-PEN-ITEM-CODE
PPC470            FROM  SAILING                          SAL
PPC470            JOIN  SAIL_PENALTY_ITEM                SPI
PPC470              ON (SPI.SHIP_CODE = SAL.SHIP_CODE)  
PPC470             AND (SPI.SAIL_ID = SAL.SAIL_ID)  
PPC470             AND (SPI.PRP_OWNER_CODE = SAL.PRP_OWNER_CODE)
PPC470             AND (SPI.PRP_OWNER_ID = SAL.PRP_OWNER_ID)
                   AND (SPI.TERM_LEVEL_CODE = :WS-TERMS-LEVEL-CODE) 
PPC470             AND (SPI.REQUEST_CABIN_CODE IN ('~','B'))
PPC470             AND SPI.LOCALITY_CODE IN ('~',:RSP50-O-LOCALITY-CODE)
PPC470             AND SPI.CURRENCY_CODE IN ('~',   
PPC470                                       :RSP06-IBR-CURRENCY-CODE)  
PPC470             AND ((SPI.AGENT_CONSUMER_CODE = '~') OR  
PPC470                  (SPI.AGENT_CONSUMER_CODE = 'C' AND  
PPC470                   :RSP06-IBR-DIRECT-IND = 'Y') OR
PPC470                  (SPI.AGENT_CONSUMER_CODE = 'A' AND  
PPC470                   :RSP06-IBR-DIRECT-IND = 'N'))  
PPC470             AND ((SPI.CONNECTION_CODE = '~')     OR  
PPC470                  (SPI.CONNECTION_CODE =  
PPC470                                       :RSP50-O-CONNECTION-CODE)) 
PPC470           WHERE  SAL.SHIP_CODE = :WS-931-SHIP-CODE   
PPC470             AND  SAL.SAIL_ID   = :WS-931-SAIL-ID 
PPC470           FETCH FIRST 1 ROW ONLY 
PPC470        END-EXEC  
PPC470        IF SQLCODE < 0
PPC470           GO TO 9150-RDMS-ERROR  
PPC470        ELSE  
PPC470           IF SQLCODE = 100   
PPC470              MOVE 8907 TO DPP42-HOLD-MSG-INDEX   
PPC470              GO TO 9200-EDIT-ERROR-MSG   
PPC470           END-IF 
PPC470        END-IF
PPC470     END-IF.  
PPC470  
PPC470     MOVE ZEROES      TO WS-RM-SUB.   
PPC470     PERFORM VARYING WS-RM FROM 1 BY 1 UNTIL WS-RM > 10   
PPC470        IF U-931-OVR-ENTITY-TYPE (WS-RM) = 'RTE'  
PPC470           MOVE WS-RM TO WS-RM-SUB
PPC470           MOVE 10    TO WS-RM
PPC470        END-IF
PPC470     END-PERFORM. 
PPC470  
PPC470     IF WS-RM-SUB <= 0
PPC470        MOVE 91 TO DPP42-HOLD-MSG-INDEX   
PPC470        GO TO 9200-EDIT-ERROR-MSG 
PPC470     END-IF.  
PPC470  
PPC470     PERFORM VARYING WS-931-SUB FROM 1 BY 1 UNTIL WS-931-SUB  
PPC470                                                 > RSP06-PAX-CNT  
PPC470          MOVE U-931-OVR-ENTITY-OWNER-ID (WS-RM-SUB)  
PPC470                          TO RSP06-PAX-RATE-OWNER-ID (WS-931-SUB) 
PPC470     END-PERFORM. 
PPC470  
PPC470     EXEC SQL 
PPC470     SELECT ALLOW_ACTUAL_CABIN_IND,   
PPC470            ALLOW_GTEE_CABIN_IND, 
PPC470            INCLUDE_AMENITY_CODE, 
PPC470            TOUR_CONDUCT_ELIGIBLE_IND

PPC470 8840-FETCH-ADD-TNC.  
PPC470     EXEC SQL 
PPC470          DECLARE ADDITIONAL_TNC_CURSOR CURSOR
PPC470           SELECT TNC.PRP_OWNER_ID
PPC470             FROM PRP_ADD_TERM_COND       PATC
PPC470             JOIN TERMS_CONDITION_MASTER  TNC 
PPC470               ON TNC.COMPANY_CODE = :WS-931-COMPANY-CODE 
PPC470              AND TNC.BASE_CURRENCY_CODE =
PPC470                                        :WS-931-CURR-CODE 
PPC470              AND TNC.TERM_COND_CODE =
PPC470                                    PATC.ADDITIONAL_TERM_COND_CODE
PPC470            WHERE PATC.PRP_OWNER_CODE = :RSP06-ENTITY-TYPE
PPC470                                (RSP06-GUEST-OFFER-CNT WS-OC-SUB) 
PPC470              AND PATC.PRP_OWNER_ID = :RSP06-ENTITY-OWN-ID
PPC470                                (RSP06-GUEST-OFFER-CNT WS-OC-SUB) 
PPC470              AND PATC.ADDITIONAL_TERM_COND_CODE > ' '
PPC470     END-EXEC.
PPC470  
PPC470     EXEC SQL 
PPC470          OPEN ADDITIONAL_TNC_CURSOR  
PPC470     END-EXEC.
PPC470  
PPC470     IF SQLCODE < 0   
PPC470        GO TO 9150-RDMS-ERROR 
PPC470     END-IF.  
PPC470  
PPC470     MOVE 0 TO WS-SUB 
PPC470               RSP06-ADD-TNC-OWNER-ID-1   
PPC470                                (RSP06-GUEST-OFFER-CNT WS-OC-SUB) 
PPC470               RSP06-ADD-TNC-OWNER-ID-2   
PPC470                                (RSP06-GUEST-OFFER-CNT WS-OC-SUB) 
PPC470               RSP06-ADD-TNC-OWNER-ID-3   
PPC470                                (RSP06-GUEST-OFFER-CNT WS-OC-SUB) 
PPC470               RSP06-ADD-TNC-OWNER-ID-4   
PPC470                                (RSP06-GUEST-OFFER-CNT WS-OC-SUB) 
PPC470               RSP06-ADD-TNC-OWNER-ID-5   
PPC470                                (RSP06-GUEST-OFFER-CNT WS-OC-SUB) 
PPC470               RSP06-ADD-TNC-OWNER-ID-6   
PPC470                                (RSP06-GUEST-OFFER-CNT WS-OC-SUB) 
PPC470               RSP06-ADD-TNC-OWNER-ID-7   
PPC470                                (RSP06-GUEST-OFFER-CNT WS-OC-SUB) 
PPC470               RSP06-ADD-TNC-OWNER-ID-8

PPC470                                (RSP06-GUEST-OFFER-CNT WS-OC-SUB).

PPC470     EXEC SQL 
PPC470          FETCH  NEXT ADDITIONAL_TNC_CURSOR   
PPC470           INTO :WS-ADD-TNC-OWNER-ID  
PPC470     END-EXEC.
PPC470  
PPC470     IF SQLCODE < 0   
PPC470        GO TO 9150-RDMS-ERROR 
PPC470     ELSE 
PPC470        IF SQLCODE = 100  
PPC470           GO TO 8840-CLOSE-ADD-TNC-CURSOR
PPC470        END-IF
PPC470     END-IF.  
PPC470  
PPC470     ADD 1 TO WS-SUB. 
PPC470     EVALUATE WS-SUB  
PPC470        WHEN 1
PPC470           MOVE WS-ADD-TNC-OWNER-ID TO RSP06-ADD-TNC-OWNER-ID-1   
PPC470                           (RSP06-GUEST-OFFER-CNT WS-OC-SUB)  
PPC470        WHEN 2
PPC470           MOVE WS-ADD-TNC-OWNER-ID TO RSP06-ADD-TNC-OWNER-ID-2   
PPC470                           (RSP06-GUEST-OFFER-CNT WS-OC-SUB)  
PPC470        WHEN 3
PPC470           MOVE WS-ADD-TNC-OWNER-ID TO RSP06-ADD-TNC-OWNER-ID-3   
PPC470                           (RSP06-GUEST-OFFER-CNT WS-OC-SUB)  
PPC470        WHEN 4
PPC470           MOVE WS-ADD-TNC-OWNER-ID TO RSP06-ADD-TNC-OWNER-ID-4   
PPC470                           (RSP06-GUEST-OFFER-CNT WS-OC-SUB)  
PPC470        WHEN 5
PPC470           MOVE WS-ADD-TNC-OWNER-ID TO RSP06-ADD-TNC-OWNER-ID-5   
PPC470                           (RSP06-GUEST-OFFER-CNT WS-OC-SUB)  
PPC470        WHEN 6
PPC470           MOVE WS-ADD-TNC-OWNER-ID TO RSP06-ADD-TNC-OWNER-ID-6   
PPC470                           (RSP06-GUEST-OFFER-CNT WS-OC-SUB)  
PPC470        WHEN 7
PPC470           MOVE WS-ADD-TNC-OWNER-ID TO RSP06-ADD-TNC-OWNER-ID-7   
PPC470                           (RSP06-GUEST-OFFER-CNT WS-OC-SUB)  
PPC470        WHEN 8
PPC470           MOVE WS-ADD-TNC-OWNER-ID TO RSP06-ADD-TNC-OWNER-ID-8   
PPC470                           (RSP06-GUEST-OFFER-CNT WS-OC-SUB)

PPC470           MOVE WS-ADD-TNC-OWNER-ID TO RSP06-ADD-TNC-OWNER-ID-9   
PPC470                           (RSP06-GUEST-OFFER-CNT WS-OC-SUB)  
PPC470        WHEN 10   
PPC470           MOVE WS-ADD-TNC-OWNER-ID TO RSP06-ADD-TNC-OWNER-ID-10  
PPC470                           (RSP06-GUEST-OFFER-CNT WS-OC-SUB)  
PPC470        WHEN 11   
PPC470           MOVE WS-ADD-TNC-OWNER-ID TO RSP06-ADD-TNC-OWNER-ID-11  
PPC470                           (RSP06-GUEST-OFFER-CNT WS-OC-SUB)
